# Current Formal Frontier Toward Proving or Disproving the Riemann Hypothesis

## Scope and evidentiary rules

This document is a present-tense topology of the RH research formalized in HTGeometry, HilbertMetric, and RHTower. It records live branch architectures, exact open cores, candidate interfaces, shared structure, and terminal no-gos. It is not chronology, research diary, or catalogue of every theorem.

Evidence surface: declarations indexed from current Lean working tree in HTGeometry, HilbertMetric, and RHTower. Cards and papers are not evidence here. Incomplete intended theorems are not reported as proved.

Lean declaration snapshot cutoff: **2026-09-06 20:22 CEST; commit `fe5b1bf7cc9c8680a962d7a62a6c24ed7bec854d`; includes the general lag counting-function law and all fixed power samples, signed half-order divisor-hyperbola cancellation, the EDQ quartic phase cutoff, the Birmingham--Mokhtari source-probe range classification, and the exact compact theta D1 expansion; incomplete declarations excluded**.

Status language:

- **Proved** means an unconditional Lean theorem.
- **Endpoint** means a proved implication or equivalence ending in RH; it does not prove its premise.
- **Receiver** means a formal object that detects or classifies off-line zeros after a source law or divisor input is supplied.
- **Producer** means a theorem derived from the literal arithmetic, theta, prime–Gamma, Möbius, or geometric source before selecting a Xi divisor.
- **Live branch label** means source, connector chain, and RH endpoint are formal, with a first independent producer still open. It does not mean the branch is mathematically independent of other labels.
- **Candidate interface label** means a novel formal receiver or source fragment exists, but the source handoff or divisor exhaustion needed for a live branch does not. It may be another realization of an existing open problem.
- **Terminal** means Lean rules out the stated producer or proves every surviving premise equivalent to RH.

No route currently proves or disproves RH.

The Liouville prime-counting-error bridge adds no route label. For the literal source
`R(N)=sum_(n<=N) lambda(n)(theta(N/n)-N/n)`, Lean proves
`R(pN)+R(N)` equals the complete contribution of cofactors not divisible by `p`, for every prime
`p`. It also proves the exact divisor collapse
`R(N)=-sum_(n<=N) lambda(n)w(n)-N sum_(n<=N)lambda(n)/n`, where `w(n)` is the sum of logarithms
of the distinct prime divisors of `n`. These identities retain rather than estimate the residual.
Any use inside E, RG, or another signed-incidence branch still needs a source-derived all-scale
bound or cancellation law for that nondivisible/weighted Liouville remainder.

The prime-block compression comparison is a closed all-scale source estimate, with no new route
label. Replace all ordinary primes in `[k^4,(k+1)^4)`, `k>=2`, by repeated copies of their geometric
mean, retaining both prime count and first logarithmic moment. Lean bounds the complete Euler-log
discrepancy, including every prime power, by
`(240|s|² exp(2|s|)+30) k^(1-4 Re(s))`. Its sum is holomorphic on `Re(s)>1/2`, and the literal
finite Euler quotients converge there to a holomorphic nowhere-zero multiplier. Thus within-block
prime locations may be compressed at controlled holomorphic-unit cost in the strict right
half-plane. This proves no zero comparison and no cancellation or order law for the retained block
counts and logarithmic means.

## Executive topology

The ledger retains **25 live branch labels** and **35 candidate interface labels**. These are indexing handles, not 60 disjoint routes. A branch is a path through shared mathematical gates; several labels can be presentations of one open source law. CFI is absorbed into CCM-FU. Its completed-Abel readout, CCM-FU checkpoint curvature, SP-E transition core, and RG weighted Chebyshev mass-error share one exact affine-slope Cesàro high-pass. The floor-selected current decomposes exactly into uniformly bounded affine memory, one signed Suzuki-curvature transform against the literal third-grade triangular reporter, and a uniformly bounded positive reset channel. Uniform control of that exact transform is equivalent to RH, so the transform is a receiver rather than an independent producer. Linear weighted-curvature first moment, natural central unit-Riesz barycenter, absolute observer-jet action, and positive same-phase shared-plane Suzuki fusion are terminal. The canonical natural-order shared-plane observable tends to positive infinity; arbitrary cutoff-dependent reordering inside the macroscopic source window does too. CCM's actual causal-depth Gamma feature is nuclear and its positive energy has exactly the feature-coordinate kernel. Fixed trace-class, finite-log-jet, zero-exponential-type functional calculus, biunitary cutoff, fixed diagonal graph, common-domain graph, canonical graph inverse, finite cyclic inverse trace, additive singular residue, and bounded rational generalized-inverse assemblies are all terminal. Any semibounded correction within fixed Hilbert--Schmidt source energy needs at least quadratic-rank escape; fixed strongly convergent projection cutoffs vanish. Local spectral scalarization also fails: source reflection is an explicit `SO(3)` conjugation of the full atomwise chiral Hermitian matrix. CCM therefore survives only through source-framed projective eigenvector incidence or indefinite cross terms, phase-twisted cross-scale fusion, a varying-domain singular/unbounded noncyclic relative trace outside weak `S1`, a cutoff-growing carrier already carrying linear source energy, or marked-halfgap arithmetic. E's nested-aperture scalar cancellation is exact, but even the two-prime aperture has both signs at explicit scales, excluding sign or monotone telescoping. Signed unit column-load control is already the Möbius half-power receiver, fixed common-spectator arity leaves near-linearly many isolated squarefree vertices, and literal complete factor-two activation histograms cannot stay inside the physical aperture. E therefore survives through growing-density product-conditioned structured cells, collective nested-aperture Ramaré cancellation, or source control of the Gamma--Hankel moving cusp. PW-B likewise cannot escape positive tomography through conditionally convergent natural ordering, fixed neutral energy, or a uniformly conditioned invariant energy bundle. The shared source--Gamma pair adds no quadratic escape: its two genuine boundaries are collinear, so every fixed complex quadratic observable is either blind or an exact shared-Ward/RH receiver. Automorphisms of the existing cyclic Gamma-six algebra untwist only trivial holonomy. Localization does not repair this selectively: no nonzero fixed principal denominator supports every grade, while the total rational field uniquely untwists every holonomy and erases the detector. Nor can an infinite commutative algebra retain a proper closed coordinate fibre while untwisting: forward fibre preservation and affine untwisting coexist exactly at trivial holonomy, with no finiteness, reducedness, or domain hypothesis. Literal rank-twelve source prefixes have strict one-dimensional determinant birth at every tick, but their raw quadratic reflection-block exponent is an exact rank-gauge coboundary. Actual directed formation times exact recovery is rank-independent; joint bilateral moderation, and bounded evaluation of the coefficient-native Laurent envelope, hold exactly at zero radial drift. The integral Laurent relative-reflection differential, its cofinal Tate assembly, and global Gamma base-change square are formal before divisor specialization. Their determinant is the exact formation-completion index; coherent primitive existence, source-marked formation properness, shell-replica closability, compact TMD descent, and index vanishing are one wall receiver, globally equivalent to RH. These are receivers, not new producers. SCE, PPM, PEP, EWQ-V, and XST are terminal. G-CE is not terminal as a whole: its nonzero affine pre-scalar prime--polar--Gamma source and forced cofinal boundary renormalization exist. This is a live G/MSC continuation, not an independent branch label; finite Laurent, finite-order entire local-curve, and finite-jet annihilators remain terminal. RG remains a live Gaussian arithmetic/Suzuki-transport architecture. MSC has literal safe-chamber `ORD + ORIENT + COF + LAND`, but Lean excludes ordinary Hilbert, Bessel/frame/Riesz, diagonal, phase-gauge, and fixed-power riggings across its singular wall. Its remaining comparison must be genuinely non-Bessel, non-power, and distributional or multivalued while retaining the forced conormal residue. RG-HF and RG-ETA are subordinate finite-Hardy and eta-current fronts. TMD and the integral curved-Tate index are presentations of Route R's same completion-birth obstruction, not independent producers. JAS cannot use multivaluedness alone: inverse source covariance into a square-zero creator forces every relation value into the vertical fibre and the quotient relation to zero. SC cannot impose the Stokes-natural skew-adjoint tie on additive two-port determinants because its central curvature has the opposite sign from normalized Xi. EDQ is a distinct candidate interface, not a live branch: its level-two and mixed-circulation data survive, but its literal moduli-two-and-three infinite exponential source product already converges unconditionally to a unit throughout `Re s>1/5`; one fixed nilpotent target proves `1/5` is the exact positive-real convergence boundary. Product convergence is therefore not a zero selector. No source theorem identifies replication with Euler dilation or orients the surviving joint signature. These closures narrow existing gates; they create no new independent live route label.

EDQ's present boundary is no longer finite assembly or convergence. Every finite mixed chronological word has an exact summable carry; finite dilation refinement retains the full within-cell correction with multiplier equal to squared Euler amplitude; and those corrections obey an exact weighted cocycle. The mixed prefix splits into ordinary centered prefix, explicit endpoint product, and dilation-endpoint pairing, with exact quadratic control of the ordinary-prefix real part. Lean then proves convergence for every ordinary cutoff when `1/2 <= Re s < 1` to the explicit sum of zero-dependent amplitude, quadratic dilation, boundary, and two absolutely convergent arithmetic remainder terms. On the wall the boundary contribution is the exact logarithmic term `-log p / 2`; incomplete final cells vanish there as well. The retained remainder tends to zero with the modulus, yet the full modulus family has an absolutely convergent Moebius inversion recovering each ordinary weighted power remainder. At every positive-real-part zeta zero, its weighted Moebius sum is an explicit nonzero boundary value of norm greater than one half; hence some modulus remainder is nonzero and total weighted absolute mass exceeds one half. For every fixed nontrivial dilation at a zero with `0 < Re s <= 1`, some corrected refinement is therefore nonzero. This terminalizes exact noncontracting scalar refinement and wholesale remainder annihilation. EDQ survives only through a source law orienting the nonzero remainder family against the explicit amplitude, dilation, and boundary terms, or orienting the joint signature whose closed deletion polygon hides degree-four circulation behind complete degree-three moment closure. The independently convergent moduli-two-and-three product, factorization-flat full energy, and finite positive endpoint models remain nonselective.

EDQ arithmetic minimum classes have unique exhaustive labels, ties, and squarefree positive support. `ArithmeticGlobalMinimumClasses` proves that their coarse pattern is normalization-driven: every strict-right parameter has `(positive,0)` labels and every strict-left positive parameter has `(0,positive)`, including at nonzeros. `EulerDeletionRemainderFirstCoefficient` proves the ordinary remainder coefficient `-s/12`; `EulerDeletionModulusFirstCoefficient` transports it by dominated convergence to the exact growing-modulus coefficient `(-conj(s)/12) sum_(n>=1)n^(-2 Re(s)-1)`. Critical-line zeros have equal positive labels because their arithmetic mean forces a finite undershoot of the limiting lower boundary. The `(0,0)` class contains no strip zero; at a strip zero both labels positive is equivalent to critical-line location. Thus all-zero minimum avoidance is closed, while demanding both minima is only a zero-fed wall classifier. `ArithmeticMinimumPhaseCompleteness` proves that real balance is only `Re(zeta(s)/(s(s+1)))=0`: every strip zero has arbitrarily nearby nonzero projected witnesses and forces an off-line nonzero projected witness. Full complex discrepancy retains the missing imaginary component but is exactly a normalized zeta receiver. Finite minimizing indices, values, and relations between moduli still lack a source law excluding off-line zero copies.

`ArithmeticPrimeSievePhase` closes the finite Möbius/coprime identity and the infinite unit-term limit for the same literal remainder. Once every small exceptional index has been removed, inserting any new prime strictly increases the imaginary part toward that limit from below, at every positive-real-part, positive-height parameter including nonzeros. At a strip zero of positive height, eventual phase nonpositivity is equivalent to `Re(s)<=1/2`; its independent source proof remains missing. The phase law is therefore a zero-fed wall receiver, not an upstream selector.

`ArithmeticSievePrimeDepth` proves the finite inverse `b_m=sum_(uv=m)mu(u)C_v`: the full coprime-sieve family and the original Möbius-weighted modulus family are mutually reconstructible on the literal source. Complete coprime observations nevertheless lose prime exponents on arbitrary coefficient sequences. A nonzero two-point transfer is invisible exactly when its endpoints have the same prime divisors. The normalized remainder may be perturbed at 2 and 4 while preserving all coprime-sieve observations, its unit, and its entire eventual tail; a fourth-multiple measurement detects the perturbation. Thus coprime-only or prime-support-only source characterization is terminal. Full divisibility depth is the exact positive survivor; it restores coefficient reconstruction but supplies no phase sign.

The actual Bernoulli successor-cell decomposition fixes the local-sign boundary. Complete cells telescope, but at height `pi/log 2` dyadic cells alternate strict imaginary sign cofinally. More strongly, the logarithmic source has an exact constant-speed scaling limit. At every fixed complete prefix and every subresonant frequency `0<lambda<2pi`, its exact first variation is strictly negative, including integer endpoint coincidences; hence that fixed prefix is eventually negative at growing cutoff. This is not uniform in turn count. At `lambda=4pi/3`, the actual second logarithmic turn remains strictly positive at arbitrarily large cutoffs. Thus universal blockwise orientation is terminal while finite-turn prefix orientation is closed. The literal normalized ordinary remainder has an explicit uniform tail sign: for `0<Re(s)<=1`, `t=Im(s)>=1`, every integer `n>=1` with `n^2>=t^3` has strictly negative imaginary part. Its exact nonnegative quartic-kernel correction bounds the scaled complex error by `|s+2||s+3|/[384(Re(s)+3)n^2]`. EDQ's remaining phase problem is cumulative orientation through the exceptional head `n^2<t^3`, tied to the zero-bearing unit remainder, or a stronger uniform prefix/tail law supplying that comparison. The proposed linear threshold `t<2*pi*n` remains open.

`ArithmeticUnitPhaseRigidity` and `ArithmeticUnitPhaseNoGo` close the precise holomorphic head-factorization proposal negatively. For the literal unit source `g(1)=conj(G)`, the identity `Im(g(1))=Im(zeta*H)` on a connected open strip domain is equivalent to `G+zeta*H=c` for one real constant. Each such domain contains at most one positive-height zero, so Hardy's analytic unbounded-zero theorem excludes both a global multiplier and every finite exhaustive chart cover. The exact positive survivor is local: a holomorphic phase germ through a positive-height strip zero exists if and only if that zero is simple and on the critical line, with an explicit divided-difference construction. Zero-free multipliers have the complete form `(c-G)/zeta`. This precise factorization would require simplicity in addition to location; it supplies no independent source orientation and does not exclude other head identities. See [the scoped no-go and paper proof](../5-failed/arithmetic-unit-phase-factorization.md) and [formal certificate](../3-formalized/arithmetic-unit-phase-classification.md).

Chronological cubic fusion (`CTF`) is a candidate interface, not a live branch. Lean derives a finite sparse cubic probe complex, its forced Hilbert completion, and exact `Gamma(s)zeta(s)` occurrence with multiplicity. Raw heat convergence, every vanishing geometric-damping schedule, and power damping with `epsilon_N log N->0` are excluded by exact positive Abel decomposition; every successful bounded array needs a persistent correction of cofinally positive `L2` size. Harmonic charge tends to zero and can be normalized away at arbitrarily small fixed budget cost. The new weighted-source Mellin theorem closes the fixed-tail boundary: for every bounded measurable weight supported below one, little-`o(t^(-1/2))` decay of `sum_n mu(n)w(nt)` forces the weight's Mellin moment to vanish at every critical zeta zero. One nonzero moment yields a recurring positive critical-scale floor. Thus a fixed compact scalar weight is not a vanishing repair unless it annihilates all critical-zero moments. CTF's arithmetic open core is a genuinely moving/nonperturbative uniformly bounded array with persistent correction and vanishing full error, or a separate all-grade source reflection/orientation law.

Source-class rigidity does not create another route label. A functional-equation- and pole-preserving polynomial quartet twist can insert off-line zeros, but Lean proves that this twist is unbounded on `Re s=2` and cannot be represented there by any absolutely convergent ordinary Dirichlet series. The polynomial-multiplier theorem excludes only the specified factor `P(s)zeta(s)` inside that class; it does not classify all divisor changes with an additional nonvanishing factor. A convergent generalized-prime modification factor is zero-free only in its stated convergence region; boundary zeros and continuation beyond it remain open. Thus boundary, finite-inertia, functional-equation, and continuous-counting countermodels apply only to the source properties they preserve and do not refute literal integer-Dirichlet or positive Euler-source architectures.

Finite Dirichlet-polynomial logarithmic laws classify the outer-law proposal more sharply. Every bounded real unit-head polynomial has a quantitative critical-line small-value bound and a two-sided dyadic comparison with its truncated Steinhaus torus Jensen mass. RG starts entirely from literal von Mangoldt and archimedean sources. Its Gaussian expression is unconditionally the canonical multiplicity-aware symmetric zero sum, obeys exact heat convolution, and is nonnegative on unbounded widths exactly under RH. Its pointwise Suzuki expression has full holomorphic/Lerch realization, exact hinge and Legendre transport, and a closed first-tail barrier. The pointwise order coordinate is also the exact weighted Chebyshev mass-error Cesàro high-pass driving CCM-FU/CFI and SP-E, up to explicit bounded archimedean and terminal corrections. Integrating the terminal correction gives a divisor-free scale potential whose monotone Dirichlet energy has increment proportional to the completed-Abel square; eventual boundedness of those increments is exactly RH. Exponential rather than bounded control now has a quantitative endpoint: a critical-Mellin resolvent bound `O(exp(alpha*t))`, for `0≤alpha<1/2`, analytically continues the regularized defect and confines every Xi zero to `Re rho≤1/2+alpha`. The complete fixed-contact hierarchy is classified. Eventual boundedness of `alignedEnergy^m/transitionCore^(m-1)` gives width `alpha_m=(m-1)/(2m)`. Order one is exactly RH; every fixed `m≥2` has strictly positive width and cannot manufacture a uniform Green bound from its algebraic inequalities. Quadratic contact is the special width `1/4`, hence only `Re rho≤3/4`. These are exact width receivers, not producers. Even a nonnegative monotone sublinear sequence under the square-root PNT envelope can have high-pass tending to positive infinity, so magnitude envelopes, positivity, monotonicity, and sublinearity do not imply the required order. Open content is signed arithmetic cancellation: a uniform Gaussian discrepancy budget on unbounded widths or control of this common pointwise high-pass through every remaining prefix and compact seam.

RG now also has a literal Gaussian Euler--continuum formation law: entire prime/continuum fields, exact current, locally uniform Xi and log-derivative convergence, safe-contour `C1` phase, zero right-half winding, and zero-free reflected transition with critical-line unit modulus. This is new source formation, not a classical receiver, but it remains inside the safe Euler chart. Its continuation across the zero-bearing strip without divisor selection is a subordinate RG front, not a new route label.

The remaining D1 sign is exactly one squared sixth-jet coupling below their product; the physical orbit occupies the unique negative Schur sheet, and every singular face satisfies one explicit lower-invariant envelope. Lean gives the exact off-shell normal-flow identity: away from the face its sole correction is lower-gap velocity times D1, while on the face the scaled determinant variation is one explicit division-free four-channel normal velocity with no eighth-jet value. On `0<x<137/2000`, the actual trajectory also lies in a refined parabolic cone and its source-positive vector field has an exact `sl₂` triple. The proposed C5--D1 slope corridor adds no reduction: pointwise on the actual orbit it is exactly `C5 slope > 0` together with native `D1 > 0`; its global form is the same conjunction. Static drift/gap/shear/source inequalities plus the projective clock cap still admit negative D1. Positive entire sources with identical jets through order eight realize every sign of the ninth-jet crossing. Monotone Schrödinger potential also fails. Global strict second-level log concavity controls a different consecutive-derivative Hankel determinant: `exp(-r^4)` satisfies it while reflected rank-three D1 is `-3760128`. Even adjoining both strict Schur diagonals and the physical negative-sheet sign does not repair the implication: `exp(-x^2-x^4/8)` satisfies that full static package with D1 `-6252`. The finite gate is therefore direct sign control of actual theta D1, equivalently the explicit normal velocity on its unique negative sheet, followed by a theta-specific source-to-divisor incidence law. A positive entire real source can have every polynomial-square reflected-Hankel form nonnegative and strict rank-three signed Hankel determinant while retaining a nonreal zero. Generic potential monotonicity, second-level concavity with the known Schur-sheet signs, the C5--D1 corridor, cofinal reflected-Hankel real-rootedness, positive Hankel-head geometry, and every regular algebra-valued contravariant carrier are terminal; any survivor must use actual theta dynamics and incidence, diagonal zero divisors, singular/indefinite data, or a changed mechanism.

FZZ-P has a literal nonlinear source flow, complete phase capacity, and an exact same-CFT wall selector. For every nonzero homology cycle `n`, the joint cycle-`n,2n` reality/passivity chamber selects zero radial monodromy; at cycles `1,2` on the canonical dyadic Mellin twist this is exactly `Re rho=1/2`. The linear replica-area tangent makes the selector quantitative: its Fourier coefficients are `-2` at zero and `2/3` at `±2n`, and its normalized response is `3/4+(exp(-4*pi*n*s1)+exp(4*pi*n*s1))/8`, which is passive exactly at `s1=0`. Canonical divisor-mode passivity for every Xi copy is therefore exactly RH, not an upstream producer. Unmarked Haar averaging keeps only the blind zero mode and satisfies the averaged chamber off wall. Pure multiplicative charge-balanced holonomy terms are twist-independent, even for arbitrary infinite towers without a convergence premise, so holonomy-only area corrections cannot produce the missing orientation. Generic tempered scalar puncture occurrence is universal at every real energy, whereas occurrence for the fixed theta-source pencil is exactly the Xi divisor. ### Primitive open gates

| Gate | One underlying mathematical question |
|---|---|
| `ORD` | Can the literal source prove the required signed order, passivity, cancellation, or current budget without absolute-value or divisor-fitted replacement? |
| `COF` | Can one source-selected cofinal family control every ground, escape, scale, aperture, or moving-degree direction rather than one finite packet or trial state? |
| `LAND` | Can source coefficients, multiplicity, prime/depth labels, and orientation survive the required singular boundary, closed-graph, determinant, or physical landing? |
| `ORIENT` | Can the source produce the reflection-odd, off-diagonal, noncentral, higher-compound, or order-sensitive incidence erased by scalar and diagonal receivers? |
| `COMP` | Can a relation proved on finite stages descend through genuine infinite completion, ancestry, or semantic identification without becoming copywise RH? |
| `DYN` | Can the full nonlinear physical source produce the selective scattering law, rather than a target-fed pole, equilibrium, or kinematic classifier? |

These gates are coupled, not a partition. `LAND` is the source/receiver firewall; `ORD`, `COF`, and `ORIENT` are the three dominant mathematical burdens; `COMP` is the infinite-limit form of the firewall; `DYN` is the physical specialization of `LAND + ORIENT`.

At coarsest honest resolution they form two coupled problems:

1. **Source production:** derive a quantitative reflection-sensitive normal datum from literal source (`ORD + ORIENT`, or `DYN`).
2. **Global faithful propagation:** carry that datum through every required scale, state, and completion without loss or copywise fitting (`COF + LAND + COMP`).

Most labels change only carrier used for one of these two problems. A mathematically independent route would need a genuinely different source producer or a genuinely different global propagation theorem, not another exact receiver.

**Sharp diagonal closure.** Lean proves Mertens' theorem `Σ_{n≤x}Λ(n)/n-log x→-γ`, the exact decomposition

**Genus-lag criterion.** The lag `Φ(L)=sum_rho exp(L(rho-1/2))/(rho(1-rho))` is absolutely convergent for every real `L`, even, real-valued, and satisfies `Φ(0)=2+gamma-log(4pi)`. For every real `X>1`, Lean proves the exact source identity

Lean now proves every converse: RH iff `Φ` is bounded, iff its real form is bounded above, iff it is bounded below, and iff `Φ` is Bohr almost periodic. Here `a_1=2+gamma-log(4pi)`, and for `n>=2`, `a_n` is the explicit Chebyshev--Mertens--trivial-zero genus error divided by `sqrt n`. **Gram and carrier forms.** The kernel `K(x,y)=Φ(log x-log y)` is positive semidefinite on every finite positive-scale packet exactly under RH. **Dense two-prime reduction and quantitative gap.** For any two distinct primes `p,q`, the additive subgroup generated by `log p` and `log q` is dense in the real line. **Single-base frontier.** Fix `b>=2`. If `a_(b^m)` is bounded above, no off-line maximal real part can be attained. Consequently boundedness forces either RH or infinitely many off-line zero copies, and more sharply either RH or, above every zero copy, another copy strictly farther right. The generating function of `a_(b^m)` has nodes `b^(rho-1/2)` with alias weights of strictly positive real part. If a right-half off-line node has a pole whose segment from zero is separated from all nonaliased poles, boundedness is impossible. Hence boundedness forces nonaliased poles to accumulate on that segment for every off-line copy: the exact shadow alternative. This is a genuine source-specific partial selector, but not yet an RH endpoint.

Lean also proves the obstruction cannot be removed by abstract positivity. For every `R>1` there are positive summable weights on nodes of modulus `<R`, accumulating at the unattained radius `R`, whose every positive-degree power sum has modulus at most one. Thus positivity, summability, bounded power sums, and nonattainment alone do not exclude the residual alternative; a successful single-base proof needs Xi-specific zero geometry, local-density/separation, or another source constraint.

**Counting-function Landau frontier.** A formal `LagWeightSystem` consists of a nonnegative weight `c(n)` with Dirichlet generator `F`, abscissa `alpha`, positive continuation width, polynomial vertical growth exponent `A<1`, and real-axis blowup `||F(alpha+t)||→∞` as `t→0+`. For every such system Lean proves

`RH ↔ exists M, forall n, c(n) != 0 -> a_n <= M`.

The coefficients `c(n)(M-a_n)` are nonnegative. Their Dirichlet series is exactly
`M F(s)-sum_rho w_rho F(s-rho+1/2)` in its initial half-plane. Landau transfer moves convergence left under not-RH, while the real-axis blowup of `F` creates a nonremovable singularity at a horizontally maximal zero; the aliased zero weight has positive real part. Controlled off-axis continuation and `A<1` make the remaining zero family locally analytic there. This isolates the mathematical property that makes a sparse sample complete.

Two concrete families are formal. The von Mangoldt system gives

`RH ↔ exists M, forall n, Lambda(n) != 0 -> a_n <= M`.

Here the support is all prime powers; prime-only boundedness still yields only the quarter strip. Independently, for every fixed integer `k>=1`, the generator `F(w)=zeta(k w)` gives

`RH ↔ exists M, forall m>=1, a_(m^k) <= M`.

Its single real pole replaces prime-gap input. Since `k` is arbitrary, every fixed-power image is complete for arbitrarily large fixed exponent. This contrasts with a single geometric progression, whose bounded power moments permit the shadow alternative. The exact open source problem is therefore to prove a common upper lag bound on any one admissible support—prime powers, one chosen `k`-th-power sequence, or another `LagWeightSystem`—not necessarily on all integers. More generally,
`Phi(L)<=M exp(aL)`, `0<=a<1/2`, gives only `Re rho<=1/2+a` unless `a=0`.



The constrained form now has an exact Fourier-multiplier presentation on the pole-vanishing sector. Multiplying a zero-mean Suzuki test by the polar symbol transports constrained ground nonnegativity to a weighted Suzuki divisor form. The polar spectral weight is bounded, reflection-compatible, and nonzero at every off-line Xi copy. Twisted two-bump convergence, the weighted Widder--Landau argument, and the pole contradiction prove that nonnegativity of the constrained ground at every positive aperture implies RH. The converse is also formal: RH gives Suzuki smooth-test positivity, and the exact multiplier-image floor criterion transfers it to the completed constrained ground. All-aperture ground nonnegativity, strict ground positivity at every finite aperture, and all-aperture `Q(Dg)≥0` are exact RH equivalences; cofinal passage and unrestricted-floor comparison are no longer part of the endpoint. Taylor localization bounds Fourier mass of functions orthogonal to a finite polynomial block, while the staircase theorem combines one certified finite low-block eigenvalue, high-tail mass, band oscillation, and a `2×2` determinant inequality into a global lower bound. This closes the analytic architecture of a finite certificate but does not supply its actual constants for every aperture.

**Open core.** Prove one source arithmetic order law. Ground form: prove the constrained ground nonnegative at every positive aperture, equivalently `|C|<=A`; all multiplier, staircase, pole-forcing, and weighted pole-exclusion machinery is downstream. Full lag form: prove `a_n<=a_1`, any global one-sided bound, or an upper bound on one fixed two-prime dense orbit. Prime-power form: prove one uniform upper bound for `a_n` on `Lambda(n)!=0`; Landau continuation now supplies the entire remaining descent to RH. Single-base form still needs both a literal-source bound and exclusion of the Xi-specific infinite nonattained/shadow chain. Gram positivity is not a separate route because its pair minor is the sharp ceiling. Prime-only boundedness stops at the quarter strip, sign-only arguments fail by `Omega+/-`, and current unconditional full-lag saving remains far above boundedness.

**Boundary.** Ground construction, cofinal landing, explicit-formula assembly, dense two-prime reduction, prime-power Landau continuation, and maximal-zero pole contradiction are closed. They do not prove source order. `D=partial^2-1/4` enforces constraints but supplies no form sign. Larger Gram matrices add no strength beyond their pair minor; integer lags have both signs infinitely often and approach the sharp ceiling, so fixed sign and strict-margin shortcuts are terminal. Abstract positive power moments cannot exclude the single-base nonattained-radius alternative. Prime-only order cannot be promoted to RH by the present short-gap estimate; it gives exactly the quarter-strip bridge. The live data are literal-source ground sign or lag ceilings, with the prime-power ceiling now the sparsest complete arithmetic endpoint proved by Lean.

### P — paired prime–Gamma signed exterior wall

**Architecture.** Exterior front: literal prime–Gamma/Stokes child kernel → paired physical Hellinger transverse form and curvature-normalized seam flux → positive-frequency free-sector passivity above the source-certified height → four-unit Ward boundary → RH. ## Terminal and retired routes

Closure applies to the named mechanism, not to every future architecture using related objects.

### FD2 — signed Xi occurrence and complete polarization lock

Literal infinite Hedenmalm theta source admits source-selected trace-corrected signed finite truncations. Their ordinary determinants equal trace-normalized Hilbert--Carleman determinants, converge locally uniformly to normalized Xi, and eventually carry exact Xi zero multiplicity on every zero-free bounded aperture. The approximation interface itself is completely classified as nonselective. The natural missing flag is also completely identified. Exact complete Green neutrality on every nonzero mode of every actual theta stage is equivalent to all-stage real divisors and, together with unconditional Hilbert--Schmidt/compact-open completion, implies RH. A root-free formulation uses only actual finite-stage matrix power traces and real-polynomial coefficients: for an `n`-point stage, nonnegativity through degree `2n+1` already forces every Schur node real without spectral weights, root enumeration, or conjugation closure. This finite-window stage property is equivalent to unrestricted Hermite positivity and modal Green neutrality; its cofinal flag closes RH. A genuinely source-derived real-stage interface does not assume this flag. The parabolic moment is defined literally from rational--Gamma mass minus the von Mangoldt contribution, with no Xi divisor coordinate. This does not contradict the FD2 lock: no theorem identifies the centered arithmetic-Gauss determinants with ordinary theta stages or proves their compact-open convergence to Xi. The construction produces real finite spectra by dropping the occurrence/landing theorem. The exact open interface is coefficient-faithful determinant identification or compact-open Xi landing for these source stages.

Every trace-faithful polarization mechanism is classified. Fixed/convergent metrics have a nonzero limiting adjoint defect. Positive escaping source-Gram/Hermitian dilation gives real finite divisors but changes the second jet and converges to a function provably different from Xi. Genuine hyperbolic Krein doubling preserves every holomorphic source power trace, but its determinant is source times Schwarz reflection and is real-divisor exactly when the source divisor is. At the actual coefficient-faithful finite theta stages, canonical-metric defect has an exact source/Stokes decomposition: a fixed nonzero rank-two pinned Green current plus a correction commutator tending to zero in operator norm. Two fixed source vectors retain quadratic values above `1/12` and below `-1/12` eventually, so the stages remain more than `1/13` in operator norm from both positive and negative cones. GTA's negative-Pell graded family gives positive quartic charge, invariant under conjugation/reindexing and zero exactly for real packets. The root-free full-defect producer is terminal. `HS²-Re tr(M²)` is exactly half ordinary skew-adjoint Hilbert--Schmidt energy and decomposes into nonnormal Schur energy plus twice squared imaginary spectral mass. ## Cross-route conclusions

### 1. Source/receiver firewall

Most routes possess a sharp RH-equivalent receiver. Rewriting it as positivity, passivity, flatness, determinant convergence, bounded energy, KMS exchange, or spectral completeness does not advance RH unless Lean derives the premise from literal source before choosing a zero. RG crosses more of this firewall than most labels. CCM-FU/CFI/SP-E cross it jointly: completed Abel and RG weighted mass-error are one scale connection whose floor mixture reduces to one signed triangular transform after bounded affine/reset channels. Weighted first-moment growth, central unit-Riesz barycenter, every regular causal-depth trace assembly, and unframed chiral spectralization are terminal. D2 boundary propagation is RH-equivalent and fails on a boundary-blind not-RH window. LI's real shape tests admit a negative fourth mode. E's bounded-density histories, fixed common-spectator arity, source-disjoint `l1` centering, stable seam inversion, and complete-prime/polynomial-prefix identification and diagonal Gamma--Hankel Möbius reconstruction cannot supply integer-prefix cancellation. HB's angular occurrence is closed at the first live order-`2m` jet and its unique entire normalization, including fixed simple inverse boundary poles; the original doubled Xi divisor is retained, and its operator/passive/clock/finite-energy radial mechanisms remain nonselective. FZZ-P has off-wall external-BRST occurrence yet no integrated mixed Ward orientation law. L has source cyclicity and a complete Burnol receiver, but every fixed reader that kills vertical ambiguity already forces trivial source holonomy. MSC has strict safe order and landing, but every stable Hilbert/fixed-rigging transport fails. These routes stop at source selection, not receiver construction.

### 2. Local exactness is abundant; global selection is scarce

Finite packets, jets, homology, local indices, multiplicities, and real-zero determinants can be realized without fixing horizontal position. Fixed autonomous order cannot retain cofinally many prime clocks. ## Normalized frontier verdict

The corpus does not support treating route labels as independent attempts. It supports overlapping labels built from six primitive gates and seven receiver complexes.

- CCM-FU/CFI, SP-E, RG, G, P, MSC, and subordinate G-CE meet in Ward/boundary receivers. - RG, STC, CSB, U, PMP/LPC, and related candidates are cofinal-selection variants. RG leaves uniform Gaussian discrepancy or bounded local production beyond its first pointwise barrier. Its Gaussian Euler--continuum formation/current/phase is exact on safe contours but still needs source-relative continuation through the zero-bearing strip. RG-HF leaves finite-to-entire rigidity; RG-ETA leaves one top order-`m-1` square-root-scale coupling. Its source budget is reusable positive salvage; other Sonin architectures are not retired. U lacks degree-uniform synchronization. - L, CDC, PSRC, RHist, PCS, TCD, JAS, WBF, HDL, TMD, TK, SC, RFHM, and R's survivors are descent variants; XST is terminal. - D2, E, SP-E, T1, T2, HC, LI, RG, HCF, EWJ, EDQ, and G-CE are signed-incidence variants; SCE is terminal. D2 needs one infinite pure-prime interior lower bound. - AKC is a candidate, not a live route. - WBF's conserved source-normal jet and equal-mass/maximal-variation fracture remain the same `ORIENT` invariant seen through Hodge curvature, Stokes excess, modular energy, transverse energy, barcode width, and prime-tail entropy. Source-class discrimination cuts across these groups without forming an eighth receiver complex. Functional equation, polar part, finite jets, finite coefficient prefixes, weighted coefficient proximity, and finite Krein inertia do not characterize the arithmetic source. A fixed continuous positive Euler pair even combines `o(x^epsilon)` counting error for every positive `epsilon` with a zero at `2/3+i`; this does not provide a discrete generalized-prime system or functional equation. Convergent generalized-prime modifications are zero-free only inside their convergence region. Polynomial-multiplier rigidity excludes the specified `P(s)zeta(s)` representation, not every divisor-changing ratio with a nonvanishing factor. Therefore countermodel no-gos apply only to the invariants they preserve, while E, T1, RG, CCM-FU, C1, and other literal-source architectures remain entitled to use exact coefficient/Euler provenance. No Lean theorem turns that provenance into `ORD`, `COF`, `ORIENT`, `LAND`, `COMP`, or `DYN`.

Consequently, recombining labels is progress only when one source theorem discharges a shared gate across multiple receiver complexes. A renamed composite or another RH-equivalent endpoint is not a route. Corpus supports three strongest live producer topologies and two formal candidate topologies:

`literal source -> reflection-odd mixed incidence -> coefficient-faithful singular completion -> cofinal whole-state/no-escape control -> existing RH receiver`.

`literal von Mangoldt/Chebyshev source -> [Gaussian discrepancy on unbounded widths | signed control of the scale connection / local formation production plus compact sign] -> [heat propagation | checkpoint/Cartan/Gamma/Xi fission] -> RH`.

`literal squarefree Möbius source -> [growing-density product-conditioned physical cells | exact nested Ramaré integer-pair incidence] -> [unsigned load dispersion + seam recurrence + interval/co-moving flux | half-power control of activation-seam polarization + seam norm + incidence-energy variation + response-defect fourth moment] -> Möbius half-power bound -> RH`.

`literal cubic pairing capacity -> canonical physical-retraction defect -> graded Hilbert/repeated-join Euler trace -> exact zeta divisor with multiplicity -> all-grade source law immune to delayed geometric surgery -> source-native fusion reflection/orientation (open) -> RH`.
